### Bar anatomy and classes (AD-2, AD-3; lengths *proposed*, HUD GDD owns them)

One linear component, three length classes keyed to the declared duration (`length_class`, D.13): the user's phrase "a linear bar at three sizes" has no repo source (ART:1043 uses "three sizes" for trail sprites); this reading is flagged in Open Questions.

| Class | MVP members | Interior fill L (art px) | Track outer | Step rate |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| SHORT | hold-to-confirm (V6, `CONFIRM_HOLD_S`) | 16 | 18 | 3 ticks/px |
| MEDIUM (D < `BAR_LONG_FROM_TICKS`) | V1 PATCH 180, `begin_cap` 90, `field_check` 120, `begin_harm` 204, `begin_revive` 180 | 24 | 26 | 3.75 to 8.5 ticks/px *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: harm was 120 and the range ended at 7.5; 204 / 24 = 8.5, and `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS < BAR_LONG_FROM_TICKS` keeps the bar MEDIUM, D.8)* |
| LONG (D >= `BAR_LONG_FROM_TICKS` 240) | V1 FULL 360, V12 240, V8 cut 240, V9 and section cut (Assembly's, cited) | 40 | 42 | 6 to 9 ticks/px |

Anatomy in HUD art px (HUD scale `H >= 3`, `H_default = max(k, 3)`, PIPE): track outer height 6 (1 border, 4 interior, 1 border) on the plate's own Ground (the bar never sits over live world pixels, HR-2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the bar is an **independent component with its own Ground** backing; it is attached to the plate's long edge by layout only. The plate hides the same frame `lit(ref)` is lost mid-channel while the bar stays until the sim's abort (up to 5 dark ticks); before this change the bar would then float over live world pixels with no Ground, breaking HR-2. A bar with no plate keeps its own 1-px-bordered Ground track)*; fill **Tungsten fixture `#FFCC66`**, drawn as filled rectangles on the art-px grid, never line primitives (4.7 `CanvasItem` lines lose their feather); needle-mark **Paper White**, 2 wide by 8 tall (1 px beyond the border at both ends), read by its protruding ends and geometry because Tungsten against Paper White is about 1.2:1; contrast Tungsten on Ground 10.8:1, Paper White on Ground 12.6:1. Strokes are at least 2 screen px at `H` = 3 by construction (HR-12). Bar length is HUD px, so it never changes with the zoom ladder step `k`, a stagger shake or any state (HR-13); the F7 guard: lengths and plate sizes are exact integer multiples of `H` at every `H` in `[H_default, 2 x H_default]` and every `k` in `[1, k_auto + 2]`. V1's class follows the PATCH or FULL choice (IA-18) from the actor's own part stock, never hp (D.13).

### Motion and animation constraints

- **Cuts, not fades** for plate show and hide, bar appear and remove, notch and outcome dim; the only animated bar motion is the cancel drain; the fill is stepped. No settle, tremor or glide on this component (ART 7.3, HR-5): a glide would read as a lying reading and the plate is not a reading. Positional stepping is exempt from the HR-13 3 Hz luminance ceiling (the exemption ART 7.3 gives the tremor; accessibility-specialist to confirm, Open Questions).
- Nothing on the HUD changes luminance faster than 3 Hz; show-starts are capped at one per `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` 20 (*proposed*); a rejection is a cut and a cue, never a shake (HR-13, IA-39). The OUTCOME dim and the notch are one-shot events.
- Work loop: 6 frames, two-handed, tool forward, stiff-weighted; **no squash, smear, overshoot or recoil on abort or completion** (ART 5.2); exaggeration is reserved for stagger, recoil and collapse frames driven by Controller or Suit, never by a verb result. The loop and `use` frames depend on the channel being active and on nothing about the target (same loop, speed and spark cadence for a healthy, damaged or hallucinated subject). Facing locks during a channel; five drawn facings, three mirrored.
- **Art lint (import):** in work, carry and stagger frames no held module, tool, limb or VFX covers the per-facing chest point `anchor_chest_<facing>` or the forward arc of the beam; sparks and scanner effects anchor at `anchor_hand_<facing>` (ART 5.1, 8.5).
- Tool sparks are short ballistic streaks falling away from the tool tip, separable from a short's fixed in-place arc pop (FT row 2) by shape and motion, not hue alone; cadence at most 3 luminance transitions per second. **Sparks are non-emissive at MVP (AD-6) and carry no light**, so a channel's Pillar 3 price is the required lit target plus the tool's noise push (IA-33), not the sparks; the decision is reversible (Open Questions 18). Reduced motion (HR-8): the fill persists (it is information), the drain becomes a snap, the abort stays a snap, the notch hold is kept, spark density may drop but sparks stay, and trail propagation (the world's answer) is never suppressed.
- Crank handle and valve wheel are world sprites driven by the player's own action and stop on the tick the channel ends, with no coast.

### Palette rules

Plate, bar track and every glyph sit on Ground `#1C2128`; borders Graphite lit `#5C6670`; text, keycap letter, tool glyph and needle Paper White `#DDE4EA` (text at least 4.5:1, glyphs at least 3:1, ART 7.6); bar fill Tungsten `#FFCC66` for every verb (the fill carries no verb or loop meaning); transient OUTCOME dim in Graphite lit. **Never** on a plate, bar, notch, rejection or confirm: Alarm Red (HR-4), Violet or Flesh (ART 4.6; the harm victim's violet fringe is the Filter's presentation outside the sprite), the loop hues (O2 Cyan, Power Amber, Coolant Ice, Fuel Ember, Hull Grey: a hue is a sentence about a system), or Chemlight `#86B896` (light colour only; the throw glyph is the deployable-light silhouette in Paper White). Tool sparks use the Tungsten family (core `#FFE2A8`, body `#FFCC66`), hue at least 38 degrees, never Fuel Ember, Power Amber or Alarm Red, and lit Hull Grey and lit Graphite under sparks stay at hue at least 36 degrees (ART 4.4 asset test). Scorch and kerf at a cut mount are Void Steel and Graphite mid, matte and hue-less. No new hue, so no new 4.7 colourblind-table entry; the Tungsten-fill against Paper-White-needle collision is carried by shape.

### Truth-channel classification (against LSG:168's closed list)

| Visual | Class | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| Prompt plate, keycap, verb text, anchor tab, NEEDS_TOOL notches (AD-8) | **Truth, meta register, never lie-capable** | A function of (object classes, tool in hand, `lit`, `in_reach`, `eligible`) only; no violet gleam, glide, tremor or flesh at any sanity stage |
| Bar, needle-mark, notch | **Truth (the actor's own elapsed time; not a reading)** | A function of declared duration and the actor's ticks; deliberately not a measure of the job in the world |
| ACK, reject, DONE and OUTCOME cues and frame sets | **Truth (transition driven)** | Edge-detected from `transition_seq`; `outcome` is unreadable before the terminal state |
| Work, carry, stagger and down frames; hand tools; chest-lamp beam; true light radius | **Truth (anchors)** | Sprites and hand tools never shift (ART 5.0) |
| Glowstick pool and source sprite | **Truth (true light radius; exposure)** | A real light the web sees |
| Hiss, drip, frost, scorch, mist, debris, puddle, cold, `AIRFLOW`, `SEAL_STRAIN`, patch weep (`27 PATCH_WEEP`) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, lamp brightness after V3, door leaf position | **Truth (consequence channel / geometry)** | The witness against any lie (LS-22); Interaction writes none of it (FT:316) |
| Door status lamp, panel indicator, breaker or valve handle position, sight-glass, gauge, inspect card | **Corruptible (module-body)** | May lie (class b); must not change on the press during the attempt window (AD-11) |
| WORN wear overlay, patch plate and its weld bead (IW-18) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* | **Corruptible (module-body)** | May lie only through the stage-3 machine-frame swap (class b); honest at stages 0-2; the plate stays drawn under a swap (IW E-IW-38); must not change on the press during the attempt window (AD-11) |
| Scanner result reading | **Open: Sanity's call** (F-3) | If lie-capable it carries the HR-5 tri-modal tell; never a binary real-or-fake icon (a P4 oracle) and never Alarm Red or green safe-danger colours |
| Phantom entity and phantom salvage lure; harm victim's fringe | **Corruptible (Filter) / per-viewer presentation** | A phantom has no subject, is never lit and never prompts; a real `junk_` item takes every verb normally |

Consequence, stated once: the bar is time the player spends, not a statement about the world. A player who watches the bar learns nothing about module-body state; one who watches the pipe learns the truth a beat behind their hands (AD-14). No "repaired" toast, sparkle or completion flash exists.

### Audio cue wiring (proposed keys; Adaptive Audio owns names, mix and the attack ramp)

UI sounds trigger through the audio event system keyed by state transition, never by truth. Cues are edge-detected from `transition_seq` and `result_seq`: a render frame may span ACK, CONFIRMED and ABORT, and only the **terminal** transition's cue plays, never skipping `ABORT`, `DONE` or `OUTCOME`. Keys: `ia_start`, `ia_loop`, `ia_cancel`, `ia_abort`, `ia_done`, `ia_nothing`, `ia_no`, plus throw, landing, cut-complete, vent and door-motor events. "Stopped", "interrupted" and "nothing happened" are **three different sounds and three different shapes** (drain; snap with notch; full bar then plate dim). A TAP sound plays at the Applied result, not the press, because a sound cannot be withdrawn. Restore plays no abort cue. A sound-caption mode for the three terminal sounds is a settings feature owed by the missing accessibility requirements (Open Questions); the visual redundancy is already specified.

### Assets implied (names per ART 8.5; sizes per ART 8.2)

`ui_prompt_plate.png` (nine-slice, anchor-tab slice), `ui_prompt_plate_notch.png` (NEEDS_TOOL border and keycap), `ui_keycap_base.png` (existing), `ui_glyph_tool_<tool>_16.png` (cutter, welder, scanner, deployable light; derived from the tool sprite profile, Paper White), `ui_bar_track.png` (3-slice stretched to L + 2), `ui_bar_needle.png`, `ui_bar_notch.png` (lengths 16, 24, 40 are Theme data, not separate files); tool sprites `tool_<cutter|welder|scanner|deployable|module>_alb.png` with tags `held_<facing>` and `use_<facing>` (60 budgeted in ART 5.7, no light); `char_crew_body_alb.png` tags `work_<facing>` and `carry_<facing>` (budgeted); `prop_glowstick_alb.png` and `light_pool_glowstick.png` (Lighting's, existing in the ART 8.3 light table); `pipe_<loop>_end` and `pipe_<loop>_cap`, a `cut` mount state for modules, a `crank` slice on every CRANK_MACHINE and an open state on every VENT_HATCH (kit confirmations, F-11); `ovl_patch_<loop>_alb` (proposed patch-plate overlay, module-body channel, with three bead-length frames for `bead_segments` 1, 2, 3; IW-18 selects) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; loose-item `prop_<item>_alb`. **Not budgeted** in ART 5.7 or 6.3 (F-2, producer): throw, pickup and drop body frames (proposal: reuse the first `work` pose plus the deployable tool's `use` tag; pickup and drop are cuts), tool-spark VFX (proposal: a tiny particle system, textures on `vfx_shared`), the patch overlay. **F-1 gap, not a disagreement:** a thrown stick has no subject and no light for `THROW_ARC_TICKS`, so as specified it vanishes at the throw and reappears at landing; the proposal, carried to Open Questions, is an Adapter-side cosmetic arc drawn from the `throw_glowstick` result event and `aim_angle` as a lit-albedo sprite visible only where something lights it (a throw into the dark still disappears, which is the item's point), snapped away at `land_tick` with no light; a restore mid-arc has no arc.

### Art review checks (retained evidence in `production/qa/evidence/`; each feeds an acceptance row)

R-1 bar classes at `H` = 3 to 6, `k` = 1 and `k_auto + 2` (AC-IA-65a); R-2 greyscale capture of ENABLED against NEEDS_TOOL (AC-IA-65c); R-3 mute capture of cancel, abort, outcome and done as four different sequences (AC-IA-100); R-4 identical-frame paired capture for a damaged and a healthy-but-believed-broken subject (AC-IA-11); R-5 25 % silhouette test of work and carry frames at five facings with the chest point clear (AC-IA-121); R-6 a valid plate candidate for every MVP interactable class at `k` = 1 and `k_auto + 2` (AC-IA-65d); R-7 palette sampling under sparks (AC-IA-122); R-8 no Chemlight pixel on any UI element (AC-IA-122); R-9 dark scene: an unlit subject has no plate and a stick landing in an unlit room shows nothing until `land_tick` (AC-IA-123); R-10 hallucination run: plates, bars and cues unchanged at drift stages 1 to 3 while module sprites and gauges show their leaks (AC-IA-123). The art bible refresh owed (ART:745 and ART:960 cite a technical-artist threshold that `lit(ref)` hysteresis, EM-22, supersedes; F-10) is listed in Cross-References, not edited here.

📌 **Asset Spec** — Visual/Audio requirements are defined. After the art bible is approved, run `/asset-spec system:interaction-system` to produce per-asset visual descriptions, dimensions, and generation prompts from this section.

## Game Feel

*Units: 1 tick = 16.7 ms (`TICK_RATE` 60); a render frame at 60 Hz is one tick, at 30 Hz two. Every number is a design target for the Adapter, HUD and animation budget; the simulation values are the Formulas section's. No loop frame rate is invented here (ART 5.2 owns the work loop's 6 frames).*

### Feel Reference

**Borrow:** Project Zomboid's timed contextual actions: a linear bar, cancelled by moving, no skill-check minigame, and the hands-free chest lamp of *Pandorum* (the beam stays on the job while both hands work). The quality borrowed is deliberate, stationary, stiff competence under pressure: pressing is instant, the work takes visible time, and leaving is always free. **Anti-reference:** a Barotrauma-style repair minigame or any QTE (a UI surface that could leak module-body truth, and a rhythm demand the "I can leave now" test forbids), and circular or confirm-dialog action wheels (HR-14).

### Input Responsiveness

| Action | Max input-to-response | Ticks | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Press `interact`, ACK pre-play | the same render frame | 0 (at most 1 frame) | keycap drop, bar at fill 0, first work pose; the hand never waits for the sim (PCI:32) |
| Applied result, CONFIRMED | at most 1 tick after the press | 1 | `queued_tick` is the next tick; the bar then fills from the committed value |
| Immediate reject, ack withdrawn | at most 1 render frame after the result event | 1 to 2 | dull `ia_no`; no shake, flash or red; capped by `PREPLAY_MAX_TICKS` 2 (*proposed*, VS-B must raise it to cover round trips) |
| TAP verb (breaker, valve, door, pickup, drop) | depress frame at the press; click and sound at the Applied result | 1 | a sound cannot be withdrawn, so it waits for the result |
| First effect of a CHANNEL | first step at `e` = `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` | 6 (100 ms) | never at the begin tick (D.11); the Graph reads it 1 to 6 ticks later |
| Release (hold) or `cancel_channel` | sim channel ends in the same P2 | 1 | the bar drain is cosmetic and never blocks a new press |
| Move intent during a channel | abort before accrual on that tick | 1 | the body moves at once; the move always wins (PC-7) |
| Hold-to-confirm (V6) | fill over `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` | 48 (0.8 s) | render time; frozen under pause; release before completion submits nothing |
| Prompt show | after the HUD debounce `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` | 13 (217 ms, *proposed*); up to 43 (717 ms) at a breach, HS-45 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* | a late prompt omits a hint, it never lies; the verb works as soon as it is focused *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the breach-time latency is stated, and measured by AC-IA-134)* |
| Prompt hide | same render frame | 0 | a prompt for an unlit or unfocused object would lie |
| `throw_glowstick` | throw pose at the press; spent at the Applied result; light at `land_tick` | 30 (`THROW_ARC_TICKS`) + 1 | the first lit table is `land_tick` + 1; no pre-play of the light |

### Animation Feel Targets

| Animation or cue | Startup | Active | Recovery | Feel goal |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Work loop (CHANNEL) | first pose held from the press (ACK) until CONFIRMED | 6-frame loop for the channel's whole accrued length, not scrubbed by progress | **0**: a cut to idle on every terminal state (no recoil, no overshoot) | stiff, committed, readable at 50 % size |
| Bar fill | appears at 0 on ACK | stepped, 3 to 9 ticks per art px by class (see Visual/Audio) | DONE holds `BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS` 12 then cuts | a clock, not a reading |
| Cancel drain | 0 | 2x fill speed (`CONFIRM_DRAIN_RATE`) | cut by the next ACK | "I let go" |
| Abort | 0 | snap to empty at once | break-notch held 12 ticks | "the world stopped me" |
| OUTCOME | bar full as DONE | plate dim one step 12 ticks, bar removed at once | 0 | "it was not what I thought" |
| TAP depress | 0 | 1 tick of keycap drop | cut | crisp and binary |
| Door attempt | press frame identical powered or not | `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` 30 of power-neutral latch strain, motor sound only while the leaf travels *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was "motor whine")* | `TAP_REARM_TICKS` 12 from the attempt's end | latency is never a power oracle |
| Throw | first work pose plus deployable `use` tag (proposed reuse) | 30-tick flight (cosmetic arc proposed, F-1) | 0 | a toss into the dark |

### Impact Moments

| Impact type | Duration | Effect | Configurable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Hit-stop | none | no verb freezes the game or the actor | no |
| Screen shake, camera impact | none | a rejection is a cut and a cue, never a shake (HR-13, IA-39) | no |
| Completion | 12 ticks | bar held full, then cut; `ia_done`; the real punctuation is the world answering (hiss thins, cold lifts, lamps dip) | `BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS` |
| "Nothing happened" | 12 ticks | plate dim one step; `ia_nothing` | same knob |
| Abort | 12 ticks | notch held; `ia_abort` | same knob |
| Inrush after a breaker close | `INRUSH_STEPS` (Graph's) | corridor lamps dip: the web saw it | Graph's |
| Controller rumble | not specified | owed to the Controller and accessibility requirements (Open Questions) | n/a |
| Time-scale slowdown | none | never | no |

### Weight and Responsiveness Profile

*Weight:* heavy and deliberate in duration (3 to 6 s jobs), light in input (one press, no combo). *Player control:* total at every tick: nothing locks movement, nothing buffers (`ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS` 0), no QTE; the commitment is readable up front because the bar's length class is fixed by the declared verb and frozen at the press. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the "readable up front" claim holds for the part-consuming V1 job only through the mandatory pre-press tell `part_tell` (a second notch on the keycap, IA-41), derived from the actor's own stock; for every other verb the class is fixed by the declaration. Without the tell the claim is false under the MVP stock rule, and the tell is a HUD story precondition.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the former claim that "the part-stocked LONG variant is visible before the press" was false, since nothing pre-press showed LONG or the part consumption. It is withdrawn: under the ready-part declaration (IA-18) the LONG variant is the player's own choice, told back to them in `focus_view.duration_class`, and the plate shows no length pip; the first time a player sees the bar's length is the press.)* *Snap:* TAP and rejection are crisp and binary; channels are analog only in the stepped bar. *Acceleration:* the action starts instantly (ACK) and ends instantly (cut); there is no ramp-up or ease-out, so the player never wonders whether a press registered. *Failure texture:* fair and diegetic: a rejection never says why (no string, CR-5) but every immediate reason is something the player can see (out of reach, dark, busy, wrong tool); a deferred outcome costs the full bar, so the failure reads as "it wasn't really broken" or "it was still too hot", never "the game wouldn't let me".

### Feel Acceptance Criteria

- [ ] Playtesters describe a nothing-happened job as "it wasn't really broken" or "it was still too hot", not "the game wouldn't let me" or "bug" (AC-IA-73).
- [ ] No tester asks for a "why can't I" message; asked how they knew a pipe was fixed they answer with a physical sign (AC-IA-73).
- [ ] Of testers who receive a deferred outcome, at least 7 of 8 explain it with a physical cause rather than "bug" or "the game wouldn't let me" *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was 4 of 5)*, so the deferred 3 to 4 s does not wear down Challenge (AC-IA-73b).
- [ ] Testers step away mid-channel when they hear something and do not report feeling punished; nobody uses "unresponsive", "laggy" or "mushy" about the press (AC-IA-124).
- [ ] The press-to-ack interval is imperceptible and a withdrawn ack never reads as a glitch (AC-IA-102).
- [ ] Cancel, abort and nothing-happened are told apart with the sound off (AC-IA-100).
- [ ] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Fewer than 1 in 5 first presses of a verb are rejected `BUSY` while the actor is moving, with the dimmed-plate tell in place; at or above that rate the Adapter-local move-then-press arm (hold a moving press for about 6 ticks, submit on stop, `ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS` unchanged at 0) is reconsidered (AC-IA-138, Open Question 46).
- [ ] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* After a TOOL push, testers perceive a consequence within about 10 s and most name it (AC-IA-136).

## UI Requirements

Interaction has **real UI** but draws none of it: the HUD (HUD & Suit Layer, designed — hud-and-suit-layer.md) draws the prompt plate and the bar from the two read views of IA-41 (`focus_view`, `channel_view`) at `world_to_hud(anchor_o)`. This section is the contract on what the views expose and what the HUD may derive; the pixels, layout and Theme are the HUD GDD's and the art-director's (Visual/Audio Requirements). Interaction adds no menu, inventory screen, crafting screen, tooltip, meter or reason text (IA-39).

| Information | Display location | Update frequency | Condition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Prompt plate: keycap glyph for `glyph_action` on the current device, plus `tr(verb_name_key)` | beside the focused object, at `world_to_hud(anchor_o)`, on the HUD layer outside the world SubViewport | once per render frame from `focus_view` | `prompt_state` ENABLED or NEEDS_TOOL, `lit(ref)` true, in reach, anchor on screen; HIDDEN otherwise |
| NEEDS_TOOL variant: notched plate and 16x16 tool glyph from `tool_required` | as above | as above | `prompt_state` NEEDS_TOOL; a press answers immediate `NO_TOOL` |
| Channel bar at class SHORT, MEDIUM or LONG (16, 24, 40 art px *proposed*) with needle-mark | attached to the plate's long edge on the side facing away from the anchor | `fill_px` per committed tick; no interpolation | the local seat's `channel_view.state` is ACK, CONFIRMED or PROGRESS; terminal frames per Visual/Audio |
| Hold-to-confirm bar (V6) | same component, class SHORT | per render frame, render-time clock | `focus_view.mode` is HOLD_CONFIRM and `input_view.hold_pm` is above 0 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the fraction is read from the Controller's `input_view`, not from "Adapter-local" state the HUD could not read)* |
| Tap-to-confirm "armed" state | needle parked at the right end, bezel thickened 1 art px | on arm and on `CONFIRM_TAP_WINDOW` expiry | Adapter policy, read as `input_view.armed`; shape, never colour alone |
| Carry-drop prompt (label `tr(drop_verb_key)`, keycap glyph for `drop_glyph_action`; *Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the literal "G . Drop" bypassed `tr()` and the device glyphs; `drop_verb_key` is the state-neutral key `verb_drop` and `drop_glyph_action` the `drop` input action, both constants of this GDD, source fields `focus_view.carry_state`, `drop_available`, `drop_anchor_o`; placement priority: the drop plate yields to the primary plate, takes the AD-5 slot nearest the foot origin that does not overlap it, and falls back to the clamped position, `UI_PLACEMENT_FALLBACK`*) | anchored at `focus_view.drop_anchor_o` (the foot origin) under the AD-5 keep-outs, as an independent second plate | as the plate | `focus_view.carry_state` is CARRYING (dimmed when `drop_available` is false); every world target except the carry-admitted rows (IA-28) is HIDDEN while carrying *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: was keyed on `carry_ref`, which the HUD may not read)* |
| Terminal cues: abort notch, DONE hold, OUTCOME dim | on the bar and plate | on `transition_seq` edges | per the terminal table in Visual/Audio |

**The views** are the IA-41 table (`focus_view`, `channel_view`; only the local seat's `channel_view` is drawn). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: **the HUD's read list is closed at four sources**, and it may read nothing else: `focus_view`, `channel_view` (including `last_result` and `prev_result`), the Controller's `input_view(seat)` `{hold_pm, armed, device_class, pressed_action}` and the result-event record `{seat, command_id, queued_tick, kind}` with `kind` in {APPLIED, REJECTED, DROPPED, SUPERSEDED} and no reason. The hold bar of V6 and the tap-to-confirm "armed" state are drawn from `input_view`; the ACK pre-play is withdrawn by the result-event record; the carry-drop prompt is drawn from `focus_view.carry_state`, `drop_available` and `drop_anchor_o` as a second plate, beside the primary plate that may carry `install_module` or `pod_hop`; the plate is dimmed while `press_blocked_moving`.)*

**Rules the HUD GDD must meet.**
- *Debounce and rate limit (D.UI-2, values *proposed* and owned by the HUD GDD).* `show = (prompt_state != HIDDEN) AND (stable_ticks >= PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS 13) AND (since_last_show >= PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS 20)`; `hide = (prompt_state == HIDDEN) OR focus_ref changed`, immediately, same render frame, no dwell. Hide is immediate because a prompt for a subject that is not lit is a lie by commission; show may be late because a late prompt omits a hint. The limiter holds show-starts to 3 Hz as full cycles *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: X9 / D91: the flash counting unit is `ceil(n_transitions / 2)`, the registry `flash_count`, i.e. full cycles, so `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` stays 20 and does not double; prompt show-starts are exempt from the per-area weighted `FLASH_MAX_PER_S` accounting as a creative-director exemption, and the strict ceiling is kept for the Alarm Red mark and under `reduce_flashing`, where this limiter is the strict 3 Hz cap)*. Worked cases: focus alternating every 6 ticks never shows; a fixture with `lit` period 12 never shows; period 30 shows at tick 13 of each on-phase at 3 Hz or slower. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* `FOCUS_STICKY_MARGIN` 0.10 (Controller) is the first defence, the debounce the safety net.
- *Bar (D.UI-1).* `progress_pm = clamp(floor((T - begin_tick) x 1000 / duration_ticks), 0, 1000)`, `fill_px = floor(progress_pm x L / 1000)`; the only inputs are elapsed ticks, declared duration, `L`, and a resumed offset (the actor's own history). PATCH 180 ticks at `L` 24: `fill_px` 0, 6, 12, 24 at elapsed 0, 45, 90, 180. Never `hp`, `state`, `repair_step_delta` or `served_pm` (the identical-frame invariant I-UI-1; AC-IA-11, AC-IA-62).
- *Ack and pre-play.* On the press show the ACK frame; on `Applied` continue into CONFIRMED with no visual change; on an immediate `Rejected`, `Dropped` or `Superseded` withdraw within one render frame; a deferred reason can never arrive as a `Rejected` (AC-IA-9). If no result is seen within `PREPLAY_MAX_TICKS` 2 of `queued_tick`, withdraw as dropped (`UI_PREPLAY_TIMEOUT`); a late `Applied` re-creates the bar at the committed value. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: `Applied`, `Rejected`, `Dropped` and `Superseded` are the `kind` of the result-event record, paired to the pre-play by `command_id` and `queued_tick`; the record carries no reason, so every `Rejected` plays the same frame set (IA-15).)*
- *Cues from the transition counter.* Track `last_seen_transition_seq` per seat; play the terminal transition's cue; skip intermediate loops (Visual/Audio).
- *Placement.* Plate position = projected anchor plus the AD-5 placement, snapped to the HUD art-px grid, held for as long as the same `focus_ref` is focused and re-validated only when invalid; none valid falls back to a clamped plate (`UI_PLACEMENT_FALLBACK`). Off-screen means the anchor projects outside the visible picture rect (no prompt, and no bar; the sim channel is unaffected); a clipped plate slides inward instead. During a channel the plate hides the same frame `lit` is lost but the **bar stays until the sim's abort** (at most 5 dark ticks): hiding it early would give an abort cue before the sim has one. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the surviving bar is drawn on its own Ground backing, an independent component, so it never floats over live world pixels when the plate is gone, HR-2.)*
- *Zoom and scale.* The anchor is re-projected every render frame; bar length and plate size are HUD px, integer multiples of `H` at every `k` in `[1, k_auto + 2]`; a zoom or HUD scale change mid-channel changes nothing in the simulation (AC-IA-101).
- *Device and glyph.* The glyph device is the Adapter's last device, classified **by event type** (never `device == 0`: 4.7 reports `DEVICE_ID_MOUSE` and `DEVICE_ID_KEYBOARD`) and latched for a non-IDLE channel or a non-zero hold, updating at the next prompt show; the bar does not reset on a device switch.
- *Localisation.* All text through `tr()` with StringName keys; a missing key renders the key and counts `UI_MISSING_KEY`; the plate grows to `PROMPT_PLATE_MAX_W_PX` (*proposed* 72 art px) then wraps to two lines, never truncating or shrinking the face; a +40 % pseudo-locale lint. Bar fill direction under right-to-left locales and CJK or Arabic face coverage are open (Open Questions). Verb labels are state-neutral (`verb_breaker`, `verb_valve`, `verb_door`, `verb_vent_hatch`).
- *Focus and input.* Prompt and bar nodes are non-focusable and mouse-ignoring (4.6 dual focus: a focusable prompt could steal gamepad focus from a menu); the in-world focus model is custom and never calls `Control` focus. The HUD reads the views once per render frame after the last committed tick; the sim never waits on the HUD.
- *Accessibility.* The three terminal outcomes differ by shape or motion, not sound alone (Visual/Audio); reduced motion per HR-8; the plate text may be exposed as a polite live region and terminal states announced as fixed phrases keyed by state, not cause (AccessKit maturity on the pinned build is unverified, Alpha scope); the missing `design/accessibility-requirements.md` is flagged.
- *Accessibility, re-review additions (2026-10-27; owner of the setting: ux-designer; the plate and bar pixels: HUD & Suit Layer).* **(a) Latched interact.** A 3 to 6 s channel hold has no alternative for a player who cannot hold a button; an **Adapter-local latched-interact setting** (press to begin, press again or `cancel` to end; the Controller's toggle-mode of PC-7 is the same two commands, so the simulation sees nothing new) is an Open Question (47) with AC-IA-149; it is not part of the simulation, is not hashed and changes no declared duration. **(b) A shape-based hold affordance.** The plate does not distinguish tap from hold, so novices learn the hold by failing; the keycap carries a shape cue from `focus_view.mode`: a TAP keycap is solid, a CHANNEL or HOLD_CONFIRM keycap carries a lower bar stub (a shape, never a hue, and never a duration or an outcome, so no oracle). **(c) The reject throttle is scoped to luminance only.** The limit of one reject presentation per seat per `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` caps the *visual* reject frame; it must not silence the `ia_no` cue, otherwise a press 150 ms after a reject is a dead input. `ia_no` has its own refractory period `NO_CUE_REFRACTORY_TICKS` (HUD-owned, proposed 6, safe range 3 to 12 [PLACEHOLDER]) and plays on every reject outside it. **(d) `reduce_flashing`.** The ACK pre-play frame, the moving tell toggling, and the reject frame all fall under the 3 Hz luminance cap and the `reduce_flashing` setting (HR-13): with it on, the ACK pre-play and the moving-tell toggle are suppressed to at most one change per `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS`. AC-IA-150 (mash and stick-chatter fixtures).
- *HUD presentation-state table (clock, cutters, priority).* The terminal holds, the cosmetic drain and the V6 hold run on different clocks; where two want the same plate or bar, the higher row wins and cuts the lower in one frame (cuts, never fades). *(Added 2026-10-27 — re-review, ui-programmer.)*

| Priority | State | Clock | Cut by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Immediate-reject hold (inset keycap, `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS`) | render time | a newer reject (restarts, rate-limited), a new ACK |
| 2 | OUTCOME dim (12 ticks) | committed ticks (`transition_seq`) | a new begin's ACK |
| 3 | DONE hold (`BAR_HOLD_DONE_TICKS`) | committed ticks | a new begin's ACK |
| 4 | Cancel drain (cosmetic rate) | render time | a new begin's ACK, an abort snap |
| 5 | V6 hold-to-confirm bar | render time, frozen under pause (`input_view.hold_pm`) | release, focus change, an immediate reject |
| 6 | Moving tell (hollow keycap) | committed ticks (`intent_octant`) | any of rows 1 to 5 draws over it and it resumes after |

- *Engine facts used (verified against `docs/engine-reference/godot/`; the reference has no `scripting.md`, `core.md` or `gameplay.md`, so nothing about progress-bar widgets, `Timer` or `_process` ordering is asserted):* 4.6 dual focus (`modules/ui.md`); 4.7 `InputEvent.device` change (`modules/input.md`); 4.7 `CanvasItem` lines carry no antialiasing feather (`breaking-changes.md`, `modules/rendering.md`), so draw the bar as filled rectangles on the art-px grid -> ADR candidate (widget choice, render-time clock source and its behaviour under pause).

| HUD-side knob (owner: HUD & Suit Layer (confirmed); art-px values need art-director sign-off) | Value | Safe range |
|---|---|---|
| `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` / `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` / `PREPLAY_MAX_TICKS` | 13 / 20 / 2 | 13-20 / 20-40 / 1-6 (VS-B at least the round trip) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)* |
| `BAR_LEN_SHORT_PX` / `MEDIUM` / `LONG` | 16 / 24 / 40 | each 12-64, ordered S < M < L |

📌 **UX Flag — Interaction System**: This system has UI requirements. In Phase 4 (Pre-Production), run `/ux-design` to create a UX spec for each screen or HUD element this system contributes to (the prompt plate in its three states, the three bar classes and terminal frames, the carry-drop prompt, the hold-to-confirm bar) **before** writing epics. Stories that reference UI should cite `design/ux/hud.md` and the interaction pattern library, not this GDD directly. The HUD GDD owns the pixels; `design/ux/hud.md` and `design/accessibility-requirements.md` do not exist yet.

**5. Registry notes (the registry-candidates stage reads the final text, F12).** New Interaction-owned constants, all [PLACEHOLDER] unless a formula fixes them: `THROW_RANGE_TILES` 3, `THROW_ARC_TICKS` 30, `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180, `FULL_CHANNEL_TICKS` 360, `CAP_CHANNEL_TICKS` 90, `CRANK_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240, `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240, `FIELD_CHECK_TICKS` 120, `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS` 204 (derived, was 120), `HARM_BP_PER_STEP` 300 (new tuned input, D36), `REVIVE_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180, `HARM_TOTAL_BP` 10000, `LIT_RECHECK_TICKS` 6, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` 600, `FULL_JOB_GRACE_S` 60, `DROP_OFFSET_PX` 24, `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` 30, `TAP_REARM_TICKS` 12, `MAX_PARTIAL_ENTRIES` 16, `MAX_GRACE_ENTRIES` 8, `BAR_LONG_FROM_TICKS` 240, `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` 120, `PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE` 24, `INTERACT_ANCHOR_MIN_SPACING_PX` 24, `MAX_PROMPT_CANDIDATES_IN_REACH` 3. New formulas (D.1 to D.13, plus D.1b `bead_segments`, added 2026-10-27): `repair_step_delta`, `act_gate`, `channel_outcome`, `throw_landing`, `drop_placement`, `partial_resume`, `full_job_charge`, `harm_step`, `noise_push`, `subject_budget`, `channel_clock`, `door_attempt`, `length_class`; `noise_push`, `subject_budget` and `length_class` are cross-system (Attention units, Emitter cap, HUD bar class), and `door_attempt` and `throw_landing` must be regenerated from the revised text (IA-24 rewrite, D.4 `s - 1` landing). This GDD is in `referenced_by` of `fairness_gate`, `W_MIN_WARNING_S`, `PATCH_DECAY_S`, `PATCH_CAP_BP` and `repair_part_for`. `THROW_RANGE_TILES` and `THROW_ARC_TICKS`, which the Controller, the Item DB and Emitter State cite by value, were registered with this GDD as source at exactly 3 and 30. Interaction-owned names added by the 2026-10-27 consistency pass (registry requests): `open_channel`, `last_salvage_T`, `admitted_while_carrying`, `ended_by`, and the cross-system knobs `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` 120 and `PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE` 24 (cited by Derelict Authoring, Web Map and Emitter State). **The starred knobs in Tuning Knobs are the cross-system registry candidates; the unstarred new constants above are Interaction-internal data and stay in this GDD's data files unless the registry-candidates stage decides otherwise.** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: one new Interaction-internal knob, `CLOSING_BLOCKED_TIMEOUT_TICKS` 300 [PLACEHOLDER], range 120-900; one new HUD-owned knob, `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS` 9, range 6-18; new Interaction-owned views `last_result` / `prev_result` on `channel_view` and the fields `press_blocked_moving`, `carry_state`, `drop_available`, `drop_anchor_o` on `focus_view`; the closed names `input_view` (Controller's) and the result-event record (kernel CR-5 projection). Changed registered values or expressions are returned as registry requests, not edited: see the row "registry entries owned by this GDD" in section 3.)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: new Interaction-owned names `HARM_BP_PER_STEP` (300, D36; `HARM_CHANNEL_TICKS` becomes derived, 204), `SUBJECT_WINDOW_HOPS` (2, [PLACEHOLDER — derive at spike], D37) and the formula `bead_segments` (D.1b, D39); changed expressions `harm_step` and `subject_budget`; changed meaning of `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` (per registration window).)*

**Owner names cited but not yet registered (the registry-candidates stage decides whether each owner registers it; this GDD uses each by the owner GDD's own identifier and never restates its value):** Life-Support Graph — `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS`, `W_MIN_STEPS`, `SEAL_STRAIN` (its `LEAK_HP`, `INRUSH_PM`, `INRUSH_STEPS` and `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC` were registered on 2026-10-27 and are cited by registry name); Player Controller & Input — `REACH_HALF_ANGLE_DEG`, `COS2_REACH`, `FOCUS_AIM_WEIGHT`, `FOCUS_STICKY_MARGIN`, `CONFIRM_TAP_WINDOW`, `ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS`, `in_reach` (a formula); Item & Module Database — `parts_per_full`. **Build relation to register on this side:** `HARM_TOTAL_BP ≥ SUIT_HP_FULL` (Suit's symbol, cited). Further new constants and formulas introduced by the Formulas and Tuning Knobs sections of this GDD are listed there with a star where they are cross-system facts.

## Acceptance Criteria

*Tags: `(auto)` headless gdUnit4 with `Kernel.step()` and stubs, BLOCKING at workflow full; `(lint)` static or data-load check; `(review)` reviewer reads text or code; `(manual)` playtest or a retained screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/`; `(spike)` measure first, then retag. IDs `AC-IA-n` (a letter suffix is a split of one draft row). "Replaces" marks the Controller stub (PCI:962-993) or neighbour criterion the row discharges. All numbers are the Formulas section's worked examples. Rows that touch an undesigned neighbour use stubs and say so.*

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: testability conventions.)* **Type, milestone and test root.** Every row has a story type, a milestone (MVP, VS-A, VS-B) and a test root, listed in the "Row classification" table at the end of this section (Logic: `tests/unit/interaction/`; Integration: `tests/integration/interaction/`; UI and Visual/Feel: retained screenshots or captures in `production/qa/evidence/`; Config/Data: `production/qa/smoke-[date].md`), per the project's Test Evidence by Story Type. **(lint) mechanisms.** A (lint) row names its mechanism: `L-DATA` is a data-load lint over `assets/data/interaction/` (schema, relation and invariant checks, proposed `tests/unit/interaction/test_verb_data_lint.gd`, CI step `lint-data`); `L-CODE` is a static scan of the source tree (grep or AST walk, proposed `tools/ci/interaction_static_lint.sh`, CI step `lint-code`); `L-REG` is a boot-registry enumeration test (proposed `tests/unit/interaction/test_boot_registry.gd`, CI step `unit`); a check with no mechanism is `(review)`, a checklist read by a reviewer, never called a lint. **Parameterisation.** Tick numbers in a row are the worked examples at the default knob values; the test computes each from the named constants (`GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS`, `FULL_CHANNEL_TICKS`, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`, ...) and never from a literal, so a retune does not break a row. **Determinism.** No row uses unseeded randomness: a former "fuzz" is an enumeration (every command type by every target class), or runs over the fixed seed list `INTERACTION_TEST_SEEDS` = {1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21, 34} in `tests/fixtures/interaction/seeds.json`, with the seed in the failure message. **Simulation versus presentation.** A row that asserts a simulation fact and a presentation fact is split: the simulation half is `(auto)` against `Kernel.step()` and stubs; the presentation half (cues, frames, ACK withdrawal) is `(auto, HUD stub)` against a HUD test double in `tests/integration/hud/`, or `(manual)` with a retained capture.

**Constants Used (so a tester without the GDD can run every row; owner in brackets).** `TICK_RATE` 60, `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6, `TILE_PX` 48, `HP_FULL` 10000, `PATCH_CAP_BP` 6000, `PATCH_DECAY_S` 240, `W_MIN_WARNING_S` 10 (registry); `LEAK_HP` 4000 (registry); `REACH_PX` 72 and `V_CARRY`/`V_WALK`/`V_RUN` 1/2/3 px per tick, `CONFIRM_HOLD_S` 0.8, `STAGGER_TICKS` 30, `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` 1800 (registry); `REACH_HALF_ANGLE_DEG` 70 (Controller's identifier, cited); `LIT_ON_PM` 150, `LIT_OFF_PM` 120, `MAX_EXPOSURE_SUBJECTS` 192, `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 8 (registry); `GLOWSTICK_LIFE_S` 150, `GLOWSTICK_RADIUS_TILES` 2 (registry); `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 (a logging threshold, not a failure line); `SUIT_HP_FULL` (Suit's symbol, cited). Interaction's own defaults are the Tuning Knobs table (for example `PATCH_CHANNEL_TICKS` 180, `FULL_CHANNEL_TICKS` 360, `CRANK_CHANNEL_TICKS` 240, `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` 600, `TAP_REARM_TICKS` 12, `DOOR_ATTEMPT_TICKS` 30, `HARM_TOTAL_BP` 10000).

### A. Registration, declaration, focus, the lit-only rule

- **AC-IA-1 (lint, L-REG)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings: the V7 decision is made (X5), so the row is unconditional; the design review had made it conditional, BLOCKING)* GIVEN the boot registry, WHEN Interaction-owned command types are enumerated, THEN they are exactly `begin_patch, begin_cut, begin_cap, begin_crank, cancel_channel, set_breaker, set_valve, set_opening, pickup, drop, throw_glowstick, field_check, begin_harm, begin_revive, top_up` (`top_up` is registered unconditionally, X5; Graph OQ 20 / Interaction OQ 4 are resolved); none named `interact` or `use_item`; each has `coalesce NONE`, `actor_binding OWNED_BY_ISSUER`, `actor_source body.state` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: kernel CR-6 / CR-7)*, `max_age_ticks` 120, not `allowed_when_paused`; only `set_opening` admits SYSTEM origin. [IA-3]
- **AC-IA-2 (review)** GIVEN a pull request that adds a verb, THEN its diff is one data row under `assets/data/interaction/verbs/`, one handler and one registration line, and the Input Adapter diff is empty. Replaces PCI:969. [IA-2]
- **AC-IA-3 (lint, L-DATA)** GIVEN the verb data and every payload schema, THEN no field takes sanity, Attention, rhythm, breach, device or hold-versus-toggle input, and payloads contain only `target_ref`, `stub_ref`, `node_id` (`top_up`'s), `target_player`, `aim_angle`, `subject_ref`, `check_kind` in {SCAN}, and (SYSTEM `set_opening` only) an explicit `area`; no field is a believed state, direction, verb choice or kind. [IA-2, IA-3, IA-11d]
- **AC-IA-4 (auto)** GIVEN a scrubber (REPAIRABLE and SALVAGE_MODULE), WHEN the welder is in hand, THEN `focus_view.verb_id` is V1; cutter: V8; bare hands: V1 with `prompt_state` NEEDS_TOOL and a press answers immediate `NO_TOOL`. Replaces the Controller's declaration stub. [IA-5]
- **AC-IA-5 (auto)** GIVEN D.2's example (anchor 70 px inside the arc), WHEN exposure is 140 with `lit` 0, THEN a press is `NOT_LIT`; at 155 `lit` turns on and the press is accepted; at 130 it stays on; at 110 it turns off; an anchor at `d^2 = REACH_PX^2` is in reach and one pixel beyond is `OUT_OF_RANGE`. Replaces the Controller's reach stub. [IA-6, D.2]
- **AC-IA-6 (auto)** GIVEN an unlit object, WHEN `drop` and `throw_glowstick` are submitted, THEN Interaction raises no `NOT_LIT`; WHEN any PLAYER-origin `begin_*`, `pickup`, `set_*` or `top_up` is submitted, THEN `NOT_LIT`; a SYSTEM-origin `set_opening` raises none (IA-24 b); WHEN `begin_patch` on ACTOR_SELF is submitted in the dark THEN Interaction raises neither `NOT_LIT` nor `OUT_OF_RANGE` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix; delegated G6, GAP: the owner adds the surface the Controller's ruled behaviour needs)*. (`toggle_beam` acceptance is Emitter State's.) [IA-6]
- **AC-IA-7 (auto)** GIVEN an unregistered ref, WHEN `lit` is queried, THEN it answers as an unlit registered ref; WHEN `interact` is pressed with no focus, THEN no command is submitted and the result-event stream is empty. **7b (auto, HUD stub)** the same press shows no pre-play and the HUD plays no cue (the Adapter's local `cue_input_ignored`, the same for every no-focus press, is the Controller's and is not a HUD cue) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix: conformed to IA-4)*. **7c (lint, L-CODE)** a release build carries no `IA_PHANTOM_PROBE`. "Unreadable" is asserted with an access spy: a double of the view records every field read by the HUD stub across a full PROGRESS channel and the assertion is that the recorded reads of `outcome` before the terminal `result_seq` all return `NONE` and that no read of `abort_cause` occurs from the HUD stub at all; `state` per the re-review restriction of IA-41.)* [IA-41]

- **AC-IA-73b (manual, playtest threshold)** GIVEN at least 8 first-time testers *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was "at least 5 (target 8)"; the forced-outcome fixture of AC-IA-73 guarantees the outcome)*, each of whom receives at least one deferred outcome (the `OUTCOME` state: a `PRECONDITION` or `BLOCKED` result after a full channel) in a 20-minute session, and is asked within 60 s of the outcome cue, with no hint of a cause, "what happened there?", THEN two observers code each answer independently as PHYSICAL (names a world cause: "it wasn't broken", "still too hot", "no power", "already running", "my eyes are going") or SYSTEM ("bug", "glitch", "the game wouldn't let me", "it didn't work"; a bare "wasted my time" with no cause); **PASS iff at least 7 of 8 answers are PHYSICAL** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: the 4-of-5 form is dropped)* (a third observer settles disagreements), no tester stops using the verb for the rest of the session, and (advisory) at least 5 of 8 are observed reading the world (looking at or listening to the pipe, stepping back, walking to a second instrument) before a later reach. **On a miss the trade-off is re-opened in this order:** (1) strengthen consequence-channel legibility (Failure & Trail and Adaptive Audio cues, never the bar); (2) shorten nominal durations inside their Tuning Knobs ranges, identically for every subject state; (3) never add a reason string, a shortened no-op or an hp-dependent duration (IA-12). Thresholds are the QA proposal [PLACEHOLDER], confirmed by the first playtest. This row turns the "We will know this was right if" criteria into a gate and protects MDA Challenge from wearing down. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: 73b also logs the honest cost of leaving, "a resumed patch costs a full bar for the tail; a half-made cut waits ten seconds": observers record every PATCH, cut, cap, crank or check that was left and resumed, whether the tester said they felt punished, and the share of resumes that fell after `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS`; if hide-then-return is common (about 1 resume in 4 expired), raise `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` toward 1200, Open Question 40.)* [Player Fantasy, Game Feel; Open Question 27]
- **AC-IA-136 (auto + manual playtest)** *(added 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated G2: **136a (auto, Attention stub, Logic):** over the fixed seed list of the Attention fixtures, a TOOL-class channel (a patch) completed or aborted with a consequence due yields that consequence within 600 ticks of the push in 100 % of the listed seeds; a percentage of random runs would contradict the determinism rule and is not used. **136b (manual playtest):**)* GIVEN at least 8 first-time testers, WHEN a TOOL-class channel (a patch) is completed or aborted and Attention has a consequence to give, THEN a consequence (a sound, a light or a creature's tell) is perceived within about 10 s of the push, and at least 7 of 8 testers name it when asked what the work did (the tell is Attention's, voiced by Adaptive Audio, IA-33); a miss re-opens the tell, never the noise push. [IA-33, Pillar 3]
- **AC-IA-138 (manual, playtest)** *(added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN at least 8 first-time testers over a 20-minute session *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was 5)*, THEN fewer than 1 in 5 first presses of a verb are rejected `BUSY` while the actor is moving (counted from the result-event record, `IA_REJECT{BUSY}` with `press_blocked_moving` set), with the dimmed-plate tell in place; at 1 in 5 or above, the Adapter-local move-then-press arm (hold a moving press for about 6 ticks and submit on stop, `ACTION_BUFFER_TICKS` still 0) is reconsidered (Open Question 46). [IA-10]

[IA-1]
- **AC-IA-100 (manual + auto frame features)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated G2 (the creative-director: per-sequence feature assertions are the gate; reviewer sorts are advisory): the five sequences must each satisfy a specific pixel feature on the retained capture, not merely differ pairwise: **drain** = the fill shortens monotonically to empty over the cosmetic drain with no break notch; **snap** = the fill is empty on the first frame and a 12-tick break notch is present; **outcome** = a full bar for the terminal tick, then the keycap and border tint for 12 ticks with the verb text unchanged; **done** = a held full bar and no tint; **reject** = no bar at all, and the keycap inset for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS`. A blind-reviewer sort of the mute captures may be run and recorded but gates nothing: with 3 reviewers the agreement takes only the values 0, 33, 67 and 100 %, so a 90 % threshold is meaningless.)*  GIVEN a mute capture of cancel, abort, outcome, done **and an immediate rejection** *(rejection added to the mute set 2026-10-27 — design review)*, keyed on `channel_view.ended_by` and never on `abort_cause`, THEN five visibly different sequences (drain; snap with a 12-tick break-notch; full bar then plate dim; held full bar; ack withdrawn with the restored keycap held one Graphite-lit step for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS`); GIVEN an immediate rejection, THEN the ack is withdrawn within one render frame with a dull cue and no shake, and GIVEN a mashed press (a press every tick for 60 ticks) THEN at most one reject presentation per `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` is shown for that seat and the luminance ceiling of 3 Hz holds (R-3). [IA-15, HR-13]

- **AC-IA-142 (auto, HUD stub)** *(added 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN `focus_view.press_blocked_moving` true, THEN the HUD double draws the plate with the hollow keycap and Paper White text and does not hide it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review: was "dims the plate one step")*; **three fixtures (re-review):** (1) a moving actor focused on a CHANNEL plate: `press_blocked_moving` true, hollow keycap; (2) a moving actor focused on a TAP plate (door, breaker) or the drop plate: `press_blocked_moving` false, the plate unchanged, and a TAP press is Applied; (3) a moving actor pressing a CHANNEL plate: answered `BUSY`, the frame shows hollow and inset together for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS` with the text token Paper White in every frame (a contrast assertion of at least 4.5:1 on the text pixels); GIVEN a rejected press, THEN the restored keycap is held for `BAR_REJECT_HOLD_TICKS` and a second rejection inside `PROMPT_MIN_CYCLE_TICKS` presents nothing new for that seat. **Row classification (type, milestone, test root, mechanism)** *(added 2026-10-27 — design review)*

Each row is listed once, under its primary classification; a row with a second half (a HUD-stub half, a capture half or a blocked integration half) says so in its own text. Milestone: **MVP** unless the group says otherwise. - **AC-IA-143 (auto + manual; D39, default applied confirmed 2026-10-28 (delegated ruling R20))** *(added 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* (auto) GIVEN banked hp `h`, THEN `bead_segments(h)` = clamp(1 + floor(2 * (`h` - `LEAK_HP`) / (`PATCH_CAP_BP` - `LEAK_HP`)), 1, 3): 4000 to 4999 gives 1, 5000 to 5999 gives 2, 6000 gives 3, monotone in `h`; GIVEN a PATCH from hp 3000 cancelled at e = 60, e = 120 and run to e = 180, THEN the banked hp is 4000, 5000, 6000, the segments are 1, 2, 3 and the Graph's re-leak times after completion are about 250 s, 340 s and 440 s; the bead frame is selected from `node_hp` by the Presenter (IW-18), Interaction supplies none of it *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*. (manual, retained capture in `production/qa/evidence/`) GIVEN captures of the three patched pipes (patch plate with bead frames 1, 2, 3) at the same distance and light, THEN they differ in bead length and in nothing else (no numeral, no colour change, no overlay, AD-14), the `27 PATCH_WEEP` weep is identical in all three captures, and at least 7 of 8 first-time testers shown any two captures say which patch will last longer (n raised from 5, re-review 2026-10-27). [D.1b, IA-19]
- **AC-IA-144 (lint + auto; D37)** *(added 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN the three MVP sections and every walkable foot position on the 8 px grid, THEN every candidate with `in_reach` true from that position is registered (inside `W`); a candidate whose room is beyond `SUBJECT_WINDOW_HOPS + 1` is unregistered; a body crossing a room boundary back and forth at the outer ring registers and unregisters each candidate at most once per crossing of the ring (hysteresis, no flapping); `post_restore` rebuilds the same registered set from body positions alone; the window sum `W_int(p)` is at most `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` at every authored body position of every registered loaded set, and `S_total` over the window never exceeds the loaded-set sum. [IA-37, D.10]

If it is meant to be a visible choice, give a diegetic difference in the weld bead (patch plate, In-World Instruments IW-18) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)* plus an AC; if not, reshape the curve deliberately. Creative direction was taken as the default above (the stated re-leak times are corrected) | agent (game-designer, creative-director); closed 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R20 | closed |

**Closed alternatives (recorded so they are not re-opened).** *DEP-1* (a block-granular ledger for incremental repair): rejected at the checkpoint, hp is the ledger and a second record could disagree after M7 clamps, decay or another seat's repair. *DEP-3* (a per-tick reach test for ACTOR targets): rejected, damage lands only at block ends after the row 4 to 6 tests, so none lands on a victim out of reach. *Part-counter oracle (M3):* closed by the part reservation (IA-18, Suit SE-41). *Noise-debt early cancel (qa-lead's option (a), Open Question 34):* rejected by the 2026-10-27 user ruling D35; the reservation is consumed by an early cancel and no TOOL debt is charged. *Sequential dispatch:* verified at KRN:63 and KRN:69, not open. *Retry owner for `CLOSING_BLOCKED`:* Interaction's `attempts[]` for both origins (IA-24). *Stowed-haul noise:* none at MVP (IA-28). *Closing a vented hatch:* a V6 act by the toggle rule (IA-22). *An Assembly-published `abort_channel(actor)` (advisor R22's provisional route):* refused by Assembly (MSA-30; registry `assembly_commands`, absent by decision) and replaced by `open_channel` and the shared record (IA-35). *Stat penalties, minigames, circular indicators, confirm-dialog wheels, a trajectory preview:* rejected by Player Fantasy, Visual/Audio and Game Feel.

**Rulings appendix** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review: the ruling codes cannot be traced to a source in the repo, because the advisor and checkpoint rulings of the 2026-10-26 run were never filed as documents. This appendix maps each code to the statement this GDD applies under it, reconstructed from the rules that cite it; it is a reading aid, not a new authority, and the rule text in Core Rules governs. The GDD is not split.)*

| Code | Statement as applied here | Where |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | Interaction is the verb layer; one declaration table (data), one command type per verb, a pull request that adds a verb is one data row, one handler, one registration line | IA-1, IA-2, IA-3, IA-23 |
| R2 | No generic `interact {target, verb}` dispatcher; `interact` is an input action, never a command type | IA-3 |
| R3 | The verb table of record (V1 to V12) and each Graph verb's mutator; V8 salvage, V12 crank as read | IA-9, IA-22, IA-25 to IA-27 |
| R4 | Class A mutators may be called from a P3 hook when each call is check-then-apply, so apply stays infallible | IA-14, IA-17, IA-36 |
| R5 | One token, one terminal state: `BLOCKED` and `PRECONDITION` are never rejects; the geometry reject is `OBSTRUCTED` | IA-10 |
| R6 | The P4 lint: no `validate` reads module-body state; a body-state precondition is an Applied outcome after the full channel | IA-11, IA-12, IA-18 |
| R7 | Phantoms: no subject, never lit, never focused, nothing submitted | IA-13 |
| R8 | Modes: TAP, HOLD_CONFIRM (destructive only, V6), CHANNEL; a channel's hold is its confirmation | IA-8, IA-22 |
| R9 | Channel clock, partial progress, incremental repair needs no ledger, the FULL-part rules | IA-14, IA-16, IA-17, IA-18 |
| R10 | Mid-channel lit and reach re-check at the `LIT_RECHECK_TICKS` cadence; the abort set and cues | IA-7, IA-14, IA-15 |
| R11 | One contextual action chosen by (object classes, tool in hand, `eligible`); the FULL-part reservation | IA-5, IA-18 |
| R12 | Pickup, drop, loose items and the salvage yield are Interaction's | IA-25, IA-28 |
| R13 | Throw: arc, landing tick, light only at landing | IA-29 |
| R14 | Field check: a lit channel that reaches Sanity as a mutator call | IA-30 |
| R15 | The friendly-harm act: an ordinary tool verb under Sanity's gate, no reward path | IA-31 |
| R16 | Noise classes; V3 and V5 toggle against the true position | IA-20, IA-33 |
| R17 | Doors: foot box, uniform attempt window, closing retry | IA-24 |
| R18 | Inspect is a permission rule only | IA-38 |
| R19 | Two published read views, integer, no strings | IA-41 |
| R20 | Subject registration lifecycle and budgets | IA-37 |
| R21 | Contention: one job per (subject, verb class); a second seat is `BUSY` | IA-34 |
| R22 | Cancel across owners; Assembly's channels live in Interaction's block | IA-35 |
| R24 | Revive has an owner so the mercy window of lethal harm is coherent | IA-32 |
| R25 | One block `interaction.state`, restore semantics | IA-36 |
| R26 | No rule reads the input device; the Adapter submits the declared type | IA-2, IA-4 |
| R27 | Loose items and carry state across restore; section-local positions | IA-28, IA-36 |
| R28 | Player verbs are exogenous and bypass the Graph's fairness gate | IA-19 |
| G1, G3, G4, G5 | Guard rails: G1 Interaction is not the doer of everything; G3 and G4 no stats, readouts or reason strings; G5 the P4 lint source | IA-1, IA-39, IA-11 |
| D10, D10b | D10: one contextual action. D10b: the accepted cost that a held part may be spent where a patch would have met the need (selection from the actor's own declaration, never hp) | IA-5, IA-18, Edge Cases B |
| F2 | The `act_gate` composite is registered as a formula | IA-6, D.2 |
